Scripting.RunJavascript.RunJavascript JavascriptCode: $'''var inputText = \"%EscapedText%\";
var inputText1 = \"%EscapedText1%\";

// Unescape
inputText = inputText
  .replace(/\\\\r\\\\n/g, \"\\n\")
  .replace(/\\\\ /g, \" \")
  .replace(/\\\\\\\\/g, \"\\\\\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\(/g, \"(\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\)/g, \")\");

inputText1 = inputText1
  .replace(/\\\\r\\\\n/g, \"\\n\")
  .replace(/\\\\ /g, \" \")
  .replace(/\\\\\\\\/g, \"\\\\\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\(/g, \"(\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\)/g, \")\");

// Parse structured inputText
var lines = inputText.split(\"\\n\");
var result = {};
var currentSection = null;

function trim(str) { return str.replace(/^\\s+/, \"\").replace(/\\s+$/, \"\"); }
function endsWithColon(str) { return str.length > 0 && str.charAt(str.length - 1) === \":\"; }

for (var i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) { // keeps original behavior (skip first line)
  var line = trim(lines[i]);
  if (endsWithColon(line)) {
    var key = line.substring(0, line.length - 1);
    var value = (i + 1 < lines.length) ? trim(lines[i + 1]) : \"\";
    i++;
    if (currentSection !== null) {
      if (!result[currentSection]) result[currentSection] = {};
      result[currentSection][key] = value;
    } else {
      result[key] = value;
    }
  } else {
    currentSection = line;
    if (!result[currentSection]) result[currentSection] = {};
  }
}

// --- CMS-specific override: Medicare Part B -> Plan Date ---
function updateFinalInfoForCMS(result) {
  try {
    var payerName = \"\";
    if (result[\"Payer Information\"] && typeof result[\"Payer Information\"] === \"object\") {
      if (result[\"Payer Information\"][\"Payer Name\"]) payerName = trim(result[\"Payer Information\"][\"Payer Name\"]);
    }
    if (!/^cms$/i.test(payerName)) return;
    if (!result[\"Coverage Info\"] || !result[\"Coverage Info\"][\"Raw Text\"]) return;

    var raw = result[\"Coverage Info\"][\"Raw Text\"];
    var lowerRaw = raw.toLowerCase();
    var idxB = lowerRaw.indexOf(\"medicare part b\");
    if (idxB === -1) {
      // Even if Part B section not found, still process Part C flag below.
      // (Continue to the Part C check)
    }

    // --- NEW: Part C active flag when CMS and MA keyword present ---
    try {
      if (result[\"Ins Type Info\"] && /MA BILL OPTION CODE/i.test(raw)) {
        result[\"Ins Type Info\"][\"IsPartCPlanActive\"] = true;
      }
    } catch (eInner) { /* swallow */ }

    if (idxB === -1) return;

    var sliced = raw.substring(idxB);
    var lines = sliced.split(\"\\n\");
    var foundBegin = null, foundEnd = null;

    function containsPlanDate(s) { return s && s.toLowerCase().indexOf(\"plan date\") !== -1; }

    for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      var line = trim(lines[i]);
      if (containsPlanDate(line)) {
        for (var j = 1; j <= 3 && (i + j) < lines.length; j++) {
          var candidate = trim(lines[i + j]);
          var pos = candidate.indexOf(\":-\");
          if (pos !== -1) {
            var dateStr = trim(candidate.substring(pos + 2));
            var parts = extractDateParts(dateStr);
            if (parts.startDate && isValidDate(parts.startDate)) {
              foundBegin = parts.startDate;
              if (parts.endDate && isValidDate(parts.endDate)) foundEnd = parts.endDate;
            }
            break;
          }
        }
        break;
      }
    }

    if (foundBegin) {
      ensureFinalInfo(result);
      result[\"Final Info\"][\"IsFound\"] = \"true\";
      result[\"Final Info\"][\"Final Begin Date\"] = foundBegin;
      result[\"Final Info\"][\"Final End Date\"] = foundEnd || \"\";
      if (!result[\"Final Info\"][\"Status\"]) {
        result[\"Final Info\"][\"Status\"] = /inactive/i.test(raw) ? \"INACTIVE\" : \"ACTIVE\";
      }
    }

    function ensureFinalInfo(obj) { if (!obj[\"Final Info\"] || typeof obj[\"Final Info\"] !== \"object\") obj[\"Final Info\"] = {}; }
  } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
}

// --- Enhancement Section ---
function extractDateParts(dateStr) {
  var parts = dateStr.split(/to/i);
  var startDate = trim(parts[0] || \"\");
  var endDate = parts.length > 1 ? trim(parts[1]) : null;
  return { startDate: startDate, endDate: endDate };
}
function isValidDate(dateStr) { return /^\\d{2}\\/\\d{2}\\/\\d{4}$/.test(dateStr); }
function parseDate(dateStr) { var p = dateStr.split(\"/\"); return new Date(p[2], p[0] - 1, p[1]); }

var finalDate = null, finalEndDate = null, isFound = false;
var dateKeys = [\"Eligibility Date\", \"Eligibility Begin Date\", \"Plan Begin Date\", \"Plan Date\"];
var allDates = [];

// Search structured sections (only mark found when at least one valid date detected)
for (var section in result) {
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(result, section)) continue;
  var sectionObj = result[section];
  if (!sectionObj || typeof sectionObj !== \"object\") continue;

  for (var i = 0; i < dateKeys.length; i++) {
    var key = dateKeys[i];
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sectionObj, key)) {
      var dateParts = extractDateParts(sectionObj[key]);
      var pushed = false;
      if (isValidDate(dateParts.startDate)) { allDates.push(dateParts.startDate); pushed = true; }
      if (dateParts.endDate && isValidDate(dateParts.endDate)) { finalEndDate = dateParts.endDate; pushed = true; }
      if (pushed) isFound = true;
    }
  }
}

// Find latest date
if (allDates.length > 0) {
  var latest = allDates[0];
  for (var i = 1; i < allDates.length; i++) if (parseDate(allDates[i]) > parseDate(latest)) latest = allDates[i];
  finalDate = latest;
}

// Coverage Info + Status
result[\"Coverage Info\"] = { \"Raw Text\": inputText1 };
var rawText = result[\"Coverage Info\"][\"Raw Text\"];
var status = /inactive/i.test(rawText) ? \"INACTIVE\" : \"ACTIVE\";

// Fallback date extraction if no valid date found and ACTIVE
if (!isFound && status === \"ACTIVE\") {
  var rawLines = rawText.split(\"\\n\");
  var coverageDates = [], coverageEndDates = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < rawLines.length; i++) {
    var line = trim(rawLines[i]);
    for (var j = 0; j < dateKeys.length; j++) {
      var keyLc = dateKeys[j].toLowerCase();
      if (line.toLowerCase().indexOf(keyLc) !== -1) {
        var dateStr = \"\";
        for (var k = 1; k <= 2; k++) {
          if (i + k < rawLines.length) {
            var candidate = trim(rawLines[i + k]);
            if (candidate.indexOf(\":-\") !== -1) { dateStr = trim(candidate.replace(\":-\", \"\")); break; }
          }
        }
        var dp = extractDateParts(dateStr);
        if (isValidDate(dp.startDate)) {
          coverageDates.push(dp.startDate);
          coverageEndDates.push(dp.endDate || \"\");
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  if (coverageDates.length > 0) {
    var latestIndex = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < coverageDates.length; i++) if (parseDate(coverageDates[i]) > parseDate(coverageDates[latestIndex])) latestIndex = i;
    finalDate = coverageDates[latestIndex];
    finalEndDate = coverageEndDates[latestIndex];
    isFound = true;
  }
}

// Final Info
result[\"Final Info\"] = {
  \"IsFound\": isFound.toString(),
  \"Final Begin Date\": finalDate || \"\",
  \"Final End Date\": finalEndDate || \"\",
  \"Status\": status
};

// Ins Type Info (defaults)
result[\"Ins Type Info\"] = {
  \"Status\": \"\",
  \"Insurance Type\": \"\",
  \"IsMedicare\": false,
  \"IsMedicareSecondary\": false, // default false (kept)
  \"IsChangeNeeded\": false,
  \"Key Word\": \"\",
  \"NewInsName\": \"\",
  \"IsPartCPlanActive\": false,   // NEW: default false
  \"User Input\": {
    \"Button\": \"\",
    \"Insurance Type\": \"\",
    \"IsMedicare\": false,
    \"IsChangeNeeded\": false,
    \"Key Word\": \"\",
    \"NewInsName\": \"\"
  }
};

// --- NEW: Medicare Secondary detection from Coverage Raw Text ---
// If \"Other or Additional Payor\" AND \"Medicare Secondary,\" are present (case-insensitive), set true.
try {
  if (/Other or Additional Payor/i.test(rawText) && /Medicare Secondary,/i.test(rawText)) {
    result[\"Ins Type Info\"][\"IsMedicareSecondary\"] = true;
  }
} catch (e) { /* swallow */ }

// CMS override (after Final Info)
updateFinalInfoForCMS(result);

// --- Manual JSON builder (safe, recursive, escapes) ---
function escapeJsonString(s) {
  return String(s)
    .replace(/\\\\/g, \"\\\\\\\\\")
    .replace(/\"/g, \"\\\\\\\"\")
    .replace(/\\r/g, \"\\\\r\")
    .replace(/\\n/g, \"\\\\n\")
    .replace(/\\t/g, \"\\\\t\");
}
function serializeObject(obj) {
  var parts = [];
  for (var k in obj) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, k)) continue;
    var v = obj[k];
    if (v && typeof v === \"object\") {
      parts.push(\"\\\"\" + escapeJsonString(k) + \"\\\":\" + serializeObject(v));
    } else {
      parts.push(\"\\\"\" + escapeJsonString(k) + \"\\\":\\\"\" + escapeJsonString(v) + \"\\\"\");
    }
  }
  return \"{\" + parts.join(\",\") + \"}\";
}

var json = serializeObject(result);
WScript.Echo(json);

var insName = \"%InsName%\";

// Replace escaped characters with actual ones
inputText1 = inputText1
    .replace(/\\\\r\\\\n/g, \"\\n\")
    .replace(/\\\\ /g, \" \")
    .replace(/\\\\\\\\/g, \"\\\\\")
    .replace(/\\\\\\(/g, \"(\")
    .replace(/\\\\\\)/g, \")\");

// Convert to lowercase for case-insensitive matching
var lowerText = inputText1.toLowerCase();
var insNameLower = insName.toLowerCase();
var normalizedInsName = insName.toUpperCase();

// Determine Coverage Status
var status = lowerText.indexOf(\"inactive\") !== -1 ? \"INACTIVE\" : \"ACTIVE\";

// Determine IsMedicare
var isMedicare = lowerText.indexOf(\"medicare\") !== -1;

// ✅ CHANGE #1: Determine IsMedicareSecondary ONLY when insName is MEDICARE
var isMedicareSecondary =
  (normalizedInsName === \"MEDICARE\") &&
  (lowerText.indexOf(\"other or additional payor\") !== -1);

// Initialize variables
var insuranceType = \"NOT FOUND\";
var newInsName = insName;
var isChangeNeeded = false;

// --- Logic for UHC / AARP / PREFERRED ---
if (normalizedInsName === \"UHC\" || normalizedInsName === \"AARP\" || normalizedInsName === \"PREFERRED\") {
    if (
        lowerText.indexOf(\"hmo-uhc preferred medicare\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"hmo-uhc preferred dual complete\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"hmopos-uhc preferred dual complete\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"hmo-uhc preferred complete\") !== -1
    ) {
        newInsName = \"PREFERRED\";
        insuranceType = \"HMO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"hmo-uhc medicaremax\") !== -1) {
        newInsName = \"PREFERRED\";
        insuranceType = \"MEDICAREMAX\";
    } else if (
        lowerText.indexOf(\"unitedhealthcare dual complete lppo\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"lppo-unitedhealthcare\") !== -1
    ) {
        newInsName = \"UHC\";
        insuranceType = \"PPO\";
    } else if (
        lowerText.indexOf(\"unitedhealthcare dual complete hmopos\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"uhc dual complete hmopos\") !== -1
    ) {
        newInsName = \"UHC\";
        insuranceType = \"HMO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"hmopos-aarp medicare advantage\") !== -1) {
        newInsName = \"AARP\";
        insuranceType = \"HMO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"lppo-aarp medicare advantage\") !== -1) {
        newInsName = \"AARP\";
        insuranceType = \"PPO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicaid\") !== -1) {
        newInsName = insName;
        insuranceType = \"MCD MGD\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"suppliment\") !== -1) {
        newInsName = insName;
        insuranceType = \"SUPPLIMENT\";
    }

    isChangeNeeded = (normalizedInsName !== newInsName.toUpperCase());
}

// --- Logic for WellCare / Simply ---
if (insNameLower === \"wellcare\" || insNameLower === \"simply\") {
    if (
        lowerText.indexOf(\"simple (hmo)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"liberty (hmo d-snp)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"giveback (hmo)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"(hmo d-snp)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"(hmo)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"(hmo c-snp)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"completeplatinum(hmod-snp)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"dsnp-dualsall exc\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"platinum (hmo d-s\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"(hmo\") !== -1
    ) {
        insuranceType = \"HMO\";
    } else if (
        lowerText.indexOf(\"simple open (ppo)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"preferred provider organization (ppo)\") !== -1
    ) {
        insuranceType = \"PPO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicaid\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"MCD MGD\";
    }
}
if (insNameLower === \"careplus\"{
      insuranceType = \"HMO\";
}
// --- Additional Categories (Fallback Rules) ---
if (insuranceType === \"Not Found\") {
    if (lowerText.indexOf(\"health maintenance organization (hmo)\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"HMO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"preferred provider organization (ppo)\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"PPO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicaid\") !== -1 && normalizedInsName === \"MEDICAID\") {
        insuranceType = \"MEDICAID\";
    // ✅ CHANGE #2: Only set MEDICARE if insName is MEDICARE
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicare\") !== -1 && normalizedInsName === \"MEDICARE\") {
        insuranceType = \"MEDICARE\";
    } else if (
        lowerText.indexOf(\"point of service (pos)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"exclusive provider organization\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"commercial\") !== -1
    ) {
        insuranceType = \"COMMERCIAL\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"networkblue\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"BRIGHTON\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"self pay\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"SELF PAY\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicaid\") !== -1 && normalizedInsName !== \"MEDICAID\") {
        insuranceType = \"MCD MGD\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"suppliment\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"SUPPLIMENT\";
    }
}

// --- Final Output Object ---
var result = {
    \"Coverage Info\": {
        \"Raw Text\": inputText1
    },
    \"Ins Type Info\": {
        \"Status\": status,
        \"Insurance Type\": insuranceType,
        \"IsMedicare\": isMedicare,
        \"IsMedicareSecondary\": isMedicareSecondary,
        \"IsChangeNeeded\": isChangeNeeded,
        \"NewInsName\": newInsName
    }
};

// --- Manual JSON Builder (kept as-is for WScript) ---
var json = \"{\";
var firstSection = true;
for (var section in result) {
    if (!firstSection) json += \",\";
    json += \"\\\"\" + section + \"\\\":{\";
    var sectionObj = result[section];
    var firstKey = true;
    for (var key in sectionObj) {
        if (!firstKey) json += \",\";
        var value = sectionObj[key];
        if (typeof value === \"boolean\") {
            json += \"\\\"\" + key + \"\\\":\" + value;
        } else {
            // Escape embedded quotes and backslashes for safe JSON output
            var safe = String(value).replace(/\\\\/g, \"\\\\\\\\\").replace(/\"/g, \'\\\\\"\');
            json += \"\\\"\" + key + \"\\\":\\\"\" + safe + \"\\\"\";
        }
        firstKey = false;
    }
    json += \"}\";
    firstSection = false;
}
json += \"}\";

// Output JSON string
WScript.Echo(json);

        DISABLE Variables.ConvertJsonToCustomObject Json: InsType_JSON CustomObject=> InsType_Object

Scripting.RunJavascript.RunJavascript JavascriptCode: $'''var inputText = \"%EscapedText%\";
var inputText1 = \"%EscapedText1%\";

// Unescape
inputText = inputText
  .replace(/\\\\r\\\\n/g, \"\\n\")
  .replace(/\\\\ /g, \" \")
  .replace(/\\\\\\\\/g, \"\\\\\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\(/g, \"(\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\)/g, \")\");

inputText1 = inputText1
  .replace(/\\\\r\\\\n/g, \"\\n\")
  .replace(/\\\\ /g, \" \")
  .replace(/\\\\\\\\/g, \"\\\\\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\(/g, \"(\")
  .replace(/\\\\\\)/g, \")\");

// Parse structured inputText
var lines = inputText.split(\"\\n\");
var result = {};
var currentSection = null;

function trim(str) { return str.replace(/^\\s+/, \"\").replace(/\\s+$/, \"\"); }
function endsWithColon(str) { return str.length > 0 && str.charAt(str.length - 1) === \":\"; }

for (var i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) { // keeps original behavior (skip first line)
  var line = trim(lines[i]);
  if (endsWithColon(line)) {
    var key = line.substring(0, line.length - 1);
    var value = (i + 1 < lines.length) ? trim(lines[i + 1]) : \"\";
    i++;
    if (currentSection !== null) {
      if (!result[currentSection]) result[currentSection] = {};
      result[currentSection][key] = value;
    } else {
      result[key] = value;
    }
  } else {
    currentSection = line;
    if (!result[currentSection]) result[currentSection] = {};
  }
}

// --- CMS-specific override: Medicare Part B -> Plan Date ---
function updateFinalInfoForCMS(result) {
  try {
    var payerName = \"\";
    if (result[\"Payer Information\"] && typeof result[\"Payer Information\"] === \"object\") {
      if (result[\"Payer Information\"][\"Payer Name\"]) payerName = trim(result[\"Payer Information\"][\"Payer Name\"]);
    }
    if (!/^cms$/i.test(payerName)) return;
    if (!result[\"Coverage Info\"] || !result[\"Coverage Info\"][\"Raw Text\"]) return;

    var raw = result[\"Coverage Info\"][\"Raw Text\"];
    var lowerRaw = raw.toLowerCase();
    var idxB = lowerRaw.indexOf(\"medicare part b\");
    if (idxB === -1) {
      // Even if Part B section not found, still process Part C flag below.
      // (Continue to the Part C check)
    }

    // --- NEW: Part C active flag when CMS and MA keyword present ---
    try {
      if (result[\"Ins Type Info\"] && /MA BILL OPTION CODE/i.test(raw)) {
        result[\"Ins Type Info\"][\"IsPartCPlanActive\"] = true;
      }
    } catch (eInner) { /* swallow */ }

    if (idxB === -1) return;

    var sliced = raw.substring(idxB);
    var lines = sliced.split(\"\\n\");
    var foundBegin = null, foundEnd = null;

    function containsPlanDate(s) { return s && s.toLowerCase().indexOf(\"plan date\") !== -1; }

    for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      var line = trim(lines[i]);
      if (containsPlanDate(line)) {
        for (var j = 1; j <= 3 && (i + j) < lines.length; j++) {
          var candidate = trim(lines[i + j]);
          var pos = candidate.indexOf(\":-\");
          if (pos !== -1) {
            var dateStr = trim(candidate.substring(pos + 2));
            var parts = extractDateParts(dateStr);
            if (parts.startDate && isValidDate(parts.startDate)) {
              foundBegin = parts.startDate;
              if (parts.endDate && isValidDate(parts.endDate)) foundEnd = parts.endDate;
            }
            break;
          }
        }
        break;
      }
    }

    if (foundBegin) {
      ensureFinalInfo(result);
      result[\"Final Info\"][\"IsFound\"] = \"true\";
      result[\"Final Info\"][\"Final Begin Date\"] = foundBegin;
      result[\"Final Info\"][\"Final End Date\"] = foundEnd || \"\";
      if (!result[\"Final Info\"][\"Status\"]) {
        result[\"Final Info\"][\"Status\"] = /inactive/i.test(raw) ? \"INACTIVE\" : \"ACTIVE\";
      }
    }

    function ensureFinalInfo(obj) { if (!obj[\"Final Info\"] || typeof obj[\"Final Info\"] !== \"object\") obj[\"Final Info\"] = {}; }
  } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
}

// --- Enhancement Section ---
function extractDateParts(dateStr) {
  var parts = dateStr.split(/to/i);
  var startDate = trim(parts[0] || \"\");
  var endDate = parts.length > 1 ? trim(parts[1]) : null;
  return { startDate: startDate, endDate: endDate };
}
function isValidDate(dateStr) { return /^\\d{2}\\/\\d{2}\\/\\d{4}$/.test(dateStr); }
function parseDate(dateStr) { var p = dateStr.split(\"/\"); return new Date(p[2], p[0] - 1, p[1]); }

var finalDate = null, finalEndDate = null, isFound = false;
var dateKeys = [\"Eligibility Date\", \"Eligibility Begin Date\", \"Plan Begin Date\", \"Plan Date\"];
var allDates = [];

// Search structured sections (only mark found when at least one valid date detected)
for (var section in result) {
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(result, section)) continue;
  var sectionObj = result[section];
  if (!sectionObj || typeof sectionObj !== \"object\") continue;

  for (var i = 0; i < dateKeys.length; i++) {
    var key = dateKeys[i];
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sectionObj, key)) {
      var dateParts = extractDateParts(sectionObj[key]);
      var pushed = false;
      if (isValidDate(dateParts.startDate)) { allDates.push(dateParts.startDate); pushed = true; }
      if (dateParts.endDate && isValidDate(dateParts.endDate)) { finalEndDate = dateParts.endDate; pushed = true; }
      if (pushed) isFound = true;
    }
  }
}

// Find latest date
if (allDates.length > 0) {
  var latest = allDates[0];
  for (var i = 1; i < allDates.length; i++) if (parseDate(allDates[i]) > parseDate(latest)) latest = allDates[i];
  finalDate = latest;
}

// Coverage Info + Status
result[\"Coverage Info\"] = { \"Raw Text\": inputText1 };
var rawText = result[\"Coverage Info\"][\"Raw Text\"];
var status = /inactive/i.test(rawText) ? \"INACTIVE\" : \"ACTIVE\";

// Fallback date extraction if no valid date found and ACTIVE
if (!isFound && status === \"ACTIVE\") {
  var rawLines = rawText.split(\"\\n\");
  var coverageDates = [], coverageEndDates = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < rawLines.length; i++) {
    var line = trim(rawLines[i]);
    for (var j = 0; j < dateKeys.length; j++) {
      var keyLc = dateKeys[j].toLowerCase();
      if (line.toLowerCase().indexOf(keyLc) !== -1) {
        var dateStr = \"\";
        for (var k = 1; k <= 2; k++) {
          if (i + k < rawLines.length) {
            var candidate = trim(rawLines[i + k]);
            if (candidate.indexOf(\":-\") !== -1) { dateStr = trim(candidate.replace(\":-\", \"\")); break; }
          }
        }
        var dp = extractDateParts(dateStr);
        if (isValidDate(dp.startDate)) {
          coverageDates.push(dp.startDate);
          coverageEndDates.push(dp.endDate || \"\");
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  if (coverageDates.length > 0) {
    var latestIndex = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < coverageDates.length; i++) if (parseDate(coverageDates[i]) > parseDate(coverageDates[latestIndex])) latestIndex = i;
    finalDate = coverageDates[latestIndex];
    finalEndDate = coverageEndDates[latestIndex];
    isFound = true;
  }
}

// Final Info
result[\"Final Info\"] = {
  \"IsFound\": isFound.toString(),
  \"Final Begin Date\": finalDate || \"\",
  \"Final End Date\": finalEndDate || \"\",
  \"Status\": status
};

// Ins Type Info (defaults)
result[\"Ins Type Info\"] = {
  \"Status\": \"\",
  \"Insurance Type\": \"\",
  \"IsMedicare\": false,
  \"IsMedicareSecondary\": false, // default false (kept)
  \"IsChangeNeeded\": false,
  \"Key Word\": \"\",
  \"NewInsName\": \"\",
  \"IsPartCPlanActive\": false,   // NEW: default false
  \"User Input\": {
    \"Button\": \"\",
    \"Insurance Type\": \"\",
    \"IsMedicare\": false,
    \"IsChangeNeeded\": false,
    \"Key Word\": \"\",
    \"NewInsName\": \"\"
  }
};

// --- NEW: Medicare Secondary detection from Coverage Raw Text ---
// If \"Other or Additional Payor\" AND \"Medicare Secondary,\" are present (case-insensitive), set true.
try {
  if (/Other or Additional Payor/i.test(rawText) && /Medicare Secondary,/i.test(rawText)) {
    result[\"Ins Type Info\"][\"IsMedicareSecondary\"] = true;
  }
} catch (e) { /* swallow */ }

// CMS override (after Final Info)
updateFinalInfoForCMS(result);

// --- Manual JSON builder (safe, recursive, escapes) ---
function escapeJsonString(s) {
  return String(s)
    .replace(/\\\\/g, \"\\\\\\\\\")
    .replace(/\"/g, \"\\\\\\\"\")
    .replace(/\\r/g, \"\\\\r\")
    .replace(/\\n/g, \"\\\\n\")
    .replace(/\\t/g, \"\\\\t\");
}
function serializeObject(obj) {
  var parts = [];
  for (var k in obj) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, k)) continue;
    var v = obj[k];
    if (v && typeof v === \"object\") {
      parts.push(\"\\\"\" + escapeJsonString(k) + \"\\\":\" + serializeObject(v));
    } else {
      parts.push(\"\\\"\" + escapeJsonString(k) + \"\\\":\\\"\" + escapeJsonString(v) + \"\\\"\");
    }
  }
  return \"{\" + parts.join(\",\") + \"}\";
}

var json = serializeObject(result);
WScript.Echo(json);

        lowerText.indexOf(\"simple open (ppo)\") !== -1 ||

        insuranceType = \"MCD MGD\";
    }
}
if (insNameLower === \"careplus\"{
      insuranceType = \"HMO\";
}
// --- Additional Categories (Fallback Rules) ---
if (insuranceType === \"Not Found\") {
    if (lowerText.indexOf(\"health maintenance organization (hmo)\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"HMO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"preferred provider organization (ppo)\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"PPO\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicaid\") !== -1 && normalizedInsName === \"MEDICAID\") {
        insuranceType = \"MEDICAID\";
    // ✅ CHANGE #2: Only set MEDICARE if insName is MEDICARE
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicare\") !== -1 && normalizedInsName === \"MEDICARE\") {
        insuranceType = \"MEDICARE\";
    } else if (
        lowerText.indexOf(\"point of service (pos)\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"exclusive provider organization\") !== -1 ||
        lowerText.indexOf(\"commercial\") !== -1
    ) {
        insuranceType = \"COMMERCIAL\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"networkblue\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"BRIGHTON\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"self pay\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"SELF PAY\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"medicaid\") !== -1 && normalizedInsName !== \"MEDICAID\") {
        insuranceType = \"MCD MGD\";
    } else if (lowerText.indexOf(\"suppliment\") !== -1) {
        insuranceType = \"SUPPLIMENT\";
    }
}

    \"Coverage Info\": {
        \"Raw Text\": inputText1
    },
    \"Ins Type Info\": {

        \"IsChangeNeeded\": isChangeNeeded,

    }

    json += \"\\\"\" + section + \"\\\":{\";
    var sectionObj = result[section];
    var firstKey = true;
    for (var key in sectionObj) {
        if (!firstKey) json += \",\";
        var value = sectionObj[key];
        if (typeof value === \"boolean\") {
            json += \"\\\"\" + key + \"\\\":\" + value;

            // Escape embedded quotes and backslashes for safe JSON output

            json += \"\\\"\" + key + \"\\\":\\\"\" + safe + \"\\\"\";
